# Add 55 reviewed retro projects across home computers, consoles, PCs, operating systems, applications and development tools.

Add 55 reviewed retro projects across home computers, consoles, PCs, operating systems, applications and development tools.

Date: 2026-10-18 · Review: ready

Snapshot check: upstream heads were not checked; pinned evidence is a historical snapshot.

## Uncertainty and next checks

- trs80-say-orchestra90 / Runtime profiles (no-evidence-found): Author specifies TRS-80 Model 4/4p with Orchestra-90 and CP/M or LS-DOS 6.3; resident driver specifically requires at least 128 KB RAM. Independent compatibility unverified.
- trs80-say-orchestra90 / AI (no-evidence-found): Unknown: no explicit AI contribution attribution established in inspected files. Absence does not establish non-use.
- trs80-montezuma-ansi-driver / Runtime profiles (no-evidence-found): Author requires Model 4, 128 KB, Montezuma Micro CP/M BIOS >=2.22; driver occupies EXMEM and disables drive M:. Source/README differ on some key mapping details, so exhaustive compatibility is not claimed.
- trs80-montezuma-ansi-driver / AI (no-evidence-found): Unknown: no explicit AI contribution attribution established in inspected files. Absence does not establish non-use.
- trs80-hxchard-fat32-driver / Runtime profiles (no-evidence-found): Author documents Models III/4 with LDOS 5.3 or Model 4 with LS-DOS 6.3, HxC emulator and FAT32/LBA SD media. VHD files must be preallocated and nonfragmented; caching uses selected extra memory banks.
- trs80-hxchard-fat32-driver / AI (no-evidence-found): Unknown: no explicit AI contribution attribution established in inspected files. Absence does not establish non-use.
- lsdos-help-extractor-generator / Runtime profiles (no-evidence-found): Windows console utility host minimum CPU/RAM unknown; native component is LS-DOS Z80 software. Format documentation exists; no independent roundtrip or byte-exact check performed.
- lsdos-help-extractor-generator / AI (no-evidence-found): Unknown: no explicit AI contribution attribution established in inspected files. Absence does not establish non-use.

- orictel-minitel-terminal / Runtime profiles (no-evidence-found): Author documents Oric-1/Atmos with 6551 LOCI/PicoWiFi modem path or Phosphoric modem/bridge environment. cc65 >=2.19 and Python >=3.8 with websockets >=12.0 are documented build/bridge prerequisites, not universal runtime hardware minima.

### Identity (reviewed)

Substantially adapted S.A.M./Bondwell speech synthesis for TRS-80 Model 4 CP/M and LS-DOS, including resident driver mode.
- https://github.com/GmEsoft/TRS80_SAY/blob/ee892043859d88185b33cf0a4fd75b5cff4e4c22/README.md
- https://github.com/GmEsoft/TRS80_SAY/blob/ee892043859d88185b33cf0a4fd75b5cff4e4c22/Say/SAY.asm

### Classification (reviewed)

Say/SAY.asm contains text-to-phoneme rules, synthesis routines, four build configurations and new LS-DOS driver integration. This is a distinct native TRS-80 port/enhancement of fdivitto/SAY, already tracked as say-bondwell-speech, rather than another copy of the unchanged Bondwell program. Root LICENSE is GPL-3.0. README explicitly preserves uncertain authorization of the historical Bondwell S.A.M. adaptation; no assertion that historical third-party rights are cleared.
- https://github.com/GmEsoft/TRS80_SAY/blob/ee892043859d88185b33cf0a4fd75b5cff4e4c22/README.md
- https://github.com/GmEsoft/TRS80_SAY/blob/ee892043859d88185b33cf0a4fd75b5cff4e4c22/Say/SAY.asm

### Source CPU (reviewed)

Inspected native Z80 assembly establishes source architecture, not a minimum runtime machine.
- https://github.com/GmEsoft/TRS80_SAY/blob/ee892043859d88185b33cf0a4fd75b5cff4e4c22/README.md
- https://github.com/GmEsoft/TRS80_SAY/blob/ee892043859d88185b33cf0a4fd75b5cff4e4c22/Say/SAY.asm

### Target CPU (reviewed)

Inspected native implementation/build establishes Z80 output architecture, not a complete minimum machine configuration.
- https://github.com/GmEsoft/TRS80_SAY/blob/ee892043859d88185b33cf0a4fd75b5cff4e4c22/README.md
- https://github.com/GmEsoft/TRS80_SAY/blob/ee892043859d88185b33cf0a4fd75b5cff4e4c22/Say/SAY.asm

### Build (reviewed)

ZMAC scripts select CP/M, LS-DOS command and LS-DOS resident driver outputs with configuration parameters. No opaque original binary inclusion found in inspected synthesis source. No upstream source was compiled or executed. No candidate software was compiled or executed in this pass; build/run/play/byte-identity flags remain unknown.
- https://github.com/GmEsoft/TRS80_SAY/blob/ee892043859d88185b33cf0a4fd75b5cff4e4c22/README.md
- https://github.com/GmEsoft/TRS80_SAY/blob/ee892043859d88185b33cf0a4fd75b5cff4e4c22/Say/SAY.asm

### Runtime profiles (no-evidence-found)

Author specifies TRS-80 Model 4/4p with Orchestra-90 and CP/M or LS-DOS 6.3; resident driver specifically requires at least 128 KB RAM. Independent compatibility unverified.
- https://github.com/GmEsoft/TRS80_SAY/blob/ee892043859d88185b33cf0a4fd75b5cff4e4c22/README.md
- https://github.com/GmEsoft/TRS80_SAY/blob/ee892043859d88185b33cf0a4fd75b5cff4e4c22/Say/SAY.asm

### AI (no-evidence-found)

Unknown: no explicit AI contribution attribution established in inspected files. Absence does not establish non-use.

### Identity (reviewed)

Native Z80 BIOS video/keyboard extension adding ANSI cursor control and text attributes to Montezuma Micro CP/M on TRS-80 Model 4.
- https://github.com/GmEsoft/TRS80_CPM_ANSI/blob/3ec065e15d19943f7ab457e4570d55016154c3be/README.md
- https://github.com/GmEsoft/TRS80_CPM_ANSI/blob/3ec065e15d19943f7ab457e4570d55016154c3be/ANSI/ansi.asm

### Classification (reviewed)

ANSI/ansi.asm implements installation checks, BIOS vector interception, extended-memory dispatch, escape-sequence parsing and keyboard remapping. Substantive original system-software extension; not a reconstruction of CP/M itself. Licensing inconsistency: root LICENSE contains GPL-3.0 while README License section and source header grant MIT terms. Preserve conflict; no single unambiguous project-wide licence inferred.
- https://github.com/GmEsoft/TRS80_CPM_ANSI/blob/3ec065e15d19943f7ab457e4570d55016154c3be/README.md

### Build (reviewed)

ANSI/a_ansi.bat invokes zmac --mras then renames CIM output to ANSI.COM. Optional tool download script exists but was not run. No upstream source was compiled or executed. No candidate software was compiled or executed in this pass; build/run/play/byte-identity flags remain unknown.

### Identity (reviewed)

TRS-80 LDOS/LS-DOS native driver that accesses virtual hard-disk files through an HxC floppy emulator and FAT32/LBA.

### Classification (reviewed)

HXCHARD.ASM implements model-specific DOS integration and caching; HxCFAT32.ASM parses FAT32 partition/cluster metadata; separate file, LBA, FDC and relocation modules accompany native DCT artifacts. Distinct software contribution around hardware, not a hardware-only repository. Root GPL-3.0; HXCHARD source preserves Timothy Mann permissive predecessor notice, requiring retained copyright and identified modifications.

### Classification (reviewed)

Actual register allocation/code generation in GEN.C and complete-looking module/project tree inspected. Self-build project enables ColdFire and floating point but disables unfinished long long/C99 features. Do not call this decompilation or newly reconstructed compiler. Root/source GPL-2.0-or-later; README states GPL/LGPL version 2, so component-specific library terms need preservation.

### Identity (reviewed)

Original Game Boy mining game by Heiko Wolf for ZFX Action XVI, with a dwarf seeking a gem before a mine collapses. Native C implementation contains movement, combat/tool durability, items, room transitions, HUD and ten map assets.
- https://github.com/der-magister/graeber/blob/f05e4ca69279192521a3e0c6981ffd154a6da88f/README.md

### Classification (reviewed)

2018 jam project updated in 2021 according to README; substantive native gameplay and maps are preserved. No full-rule completion or hardware run verified. GPL-2.0 root licence, with source headers permitting GPL version 2 or later.
- https://github.com/der-magister/graeber/blob/f05e4ca69279192521a3e0c6981ffd154a6da88f/README.md

Makefile delegates to compile.sh, which uses /opt/gbdk/bin/lcc to compile graphics/gameplay banks and link graeber.gb, then calls romusage. Its cleanup and generated-output operations were not run. ### Identity (reviewed)

Original native Game Boy assembly pirate-exploration game with seeded procedural ocean generation, streamed tile updates, sailing/combat, port economy, shore exploration and two-slot battery saves. Substantial world/combat/save implementations are present; the browser directory emulates the produced ROM and is not the game implementation.

Author claims DMG/CGB/AGB/SGB/Pocket compatibility and finished treasure quest. These claims and procedural-world/rotation-save robustness remain unverified. Original Game Boy Color audiovisual demo with multi-scene native C/assembly presentation, DMA-driven tile animation, scanline palette effects, pseudo-3D tower/road and roto-zoom effects. Actual scene code and a 16-bank ROM are present, beyond a static screenshot/example collection.

### Identity (reviewed)

From-scratch native C reimplementation of RetroSouls’ ZX Spectrum GLUF for SMS, Game Gear and Game Boy Color, built on CrossZGB. Inspected coroutine-driven player/level state implements charging, floor circuits, lifts, enemies, level progression and access codes; original level assets are converted by project-specific tools.
- https://github.com/untoxa/gluf/blob/0fa3c51177a75487f19d5c06ed2f808893ade3f7/README.md
- https://github.com/untoxa/gluf/blob/0fa3c51177a75487f19d5c06ed2f808893ade3f7/src/Makefile

### Classification (reviewed)

25-level game and per-platform art are documented. MakefileCustom identifies binary level data supplied by original author Denis Grachev; this is data reuse and native reimplementation, not evidence of original Z80 code recovery. MIT root licence for this project; RetroSouls original art/level/music origins are credited separately and not exhaustively rights-audited.
- https://github.com/untoxa/gluf/blob/0fa3c51177a75487f19d5c06ed2f808893ade3f7/README.md

Original ZX Spectrum level assets supplied by Denis Grachev are processed as data; no predecessor executable/CPU implementation is analyzed. Source CPU is not applicable to this bounded asset/native-reimplementation scope.

### Identity (reviewed)

DOS ASSIGN-compatible drive remapping implemented with C loader and resident assembly intercepting INT21 and absolute disk INT25/26.
- https://github.com/FDOS/assign/blob/c71cfe62a264da529b8d0fa8052bccf46d2bd84b/assign.c
- https://github.com/FDOS/assign/blob/c71cfe62a264da529b8d0fa8052bccf46d2bd84b/asgn_tsr.asm

### Classification (reviewed)

Actual interrupt-chaining/drive-translation table logic and loader inspection establish substantive work. Old manual confusingly says program is no TSR while code explicitly separates resident module and loader; record the actual resident mechanism. Source documents intended MS-DOS3.0–6.22, warnings on later versions, and unverified other-DOS compatibility. Both C and assembly explicitly GNU GPLv1-or-later; do not assume GPLv2-only merely because sibling FreeDOS packages use it.
- https://github.com/FDOS/assign/blob/c71cfe62a264da529b8d0fa8052bccf46d2bd84b/assign.c

### Identity (reviewed)

Independent Sun-2 workstation emulator with MMU, SCSI, framebuffer/keyboard and experimental 3C400 network-device implementations.
- https://github.com/lisper/emulator-sun-2/blob/0fb1815cb2473a4138468db21eae20b0479fafe3/README.md
- https://github.com/lisper/emulator-sun-2/blob/0fb1815cb2473a4138468db21eae20b0479fafe3/README.NetBSD

### Classification (reviewed)

Author reports SunOS 2.0/3.2/3.5 booting, with shaky SCSI tape and alpha Ethernet support. README explicitly describes independently rewriting after studying TME; no completeness inferred. Mixed restrictive licensing: Sun-2 source headers are GPL-2.0-or-later, but bundled Musashi 3.3 m68k/m68k.h permits only non-commercial use absent negotiation. Bundled guest OS/media rights are separate; no blanket open-source claim.
- https://github.com/lisper/emulator-sun-2/blob/0fb1815cb2473a4138468db21eae20b0479fafe3/README.md
- https://github.com/lisper/emulator-sun-2/blob/0fb1815cb2473a4138468db21eae20b0479fafe3/README.NetBSD

## Research event notes

Broad discovery followed platform catalogues, developer portfolios, source-preservation and tooling links, with programmatic URL screening before detailed inspection. This is a diverse bounded pass, not an exhaustive inventory of every platform. Quality and completeness caveats remain explicit; preserved source, native reconstruction, homebrew and modern tooling are distinguished. No candidate code was executed. All earlier catalogue records are preserved.
